# SuperSub Bundles in Mudae

## List of All Known Superbundle-Subbundle Pairs

General ($disableblock-ed) bundles (eg: Anime, Manga...) are excluded from calculations.

Number of known pairs: 16568 => **17843**

Number of unique super-bundles: 1912 => **2001** (27.9% of all bundles)

Number of unique sub-bundles: 4546 => **4877** (68.0%)
